==================
marker: DRC Marker
------------------
area: area, Area of the marker as defined by the LEF MACRO SIZE or OVERLAP information
box: rect, Bounding box for marker
box_area: area, Area of bounding box for marker
box_ll: pt, Lower left (ll) of bounding box for marker
box_llx: coord, Lower left X (llx) of bounding box for marker
box_lly: coord, Lower left Y (lly) of bounding box for marker
box_size: pt, Size of bounding box for marker
box_sizex: coord, Size X of bounding box for marker
box_sizey: coord, Size Y of bounding box for marker
box_ur: pt, Upper right (ur) of bounding box for marker
box_urx: coord, Upper Right X (urx) of bounding box for marker
box_ury: coord, Upper Right Y (ury) of bounding box for marker
isFalse(settable): bool, Indicates the marker is marked as false violation by user.
isHidden(settable): bool, Indicates the marker is hidden by user in violation browser.
layer: obj(layer), Pointer to layer
message: string, DRC marker message
messageId: int, DRC marker message ID
